[Optimization Guide] Support targeted use-case uninstallation

Update `ManifestBrokerState::SetUseCaseRequested()` to enable asset
eviction and recompute asset priorities when `requested` is false. This
allows callers to uninstall components associated with a specific use
case when no longer needed, while safely preserving assets shared with
other active use cases.

SurfaceEmbed: Propagate embedder occlusion as OCCLUDED, not HIDDEN

SurfaceEmbedConnectorImpl::UpdateChildVisibility() treated any embedder
visibility other than VISIBLE as hidden. As a result, when the embedder
WebContents was merely occluded, the embedded child WebContents was
marked HIDDEN instead of OCCLUDED.

Distinguish between a hidden and an occluded embedder: a hidden embedder
still hides the child, while an occluded embedder now occludes the
child, unless the <embed> element itself is not rendered, in which case
the child stays hidden. This matches how regular inner WebContents
inherit their outer WebContents' visibility.

Add browser tests covering the occluded, shown, and hidden transitions,
including propagation through nested embeds (A -> B -> C).

[ChroMenu] Add AppMenuSearchController::Delegate

Move the search logic from ActionAppMenu into AppMenuSearchController.
The controller now listens to the search bar, keeps the results tree,
and tells ActionAppMenu what to show through a Delegate. Search()
returns a new tree, so the old one is freed only after the menu replaces
its rows.

This prepares for keeping the results for short queries and adding a
clear button.

No behavior change.

🌺 [Android] Drop VFS caches after root push in force_main_user mode

When force_main_user is enabled (now the default on Android desktop HSUM
devices since crrev.com/c/8584255), push_test_data resolves
/sdcard/chromium_tests_root to /data/media/10/chromium_tests_root and
extracts changed files directly onto the lower /data/media/10 filesystem
as root.

Because test processes read test data via the upper /sdcard
(/storage/emulated/10) MediaProvider FUSE mount, writing directly to
/data/media/10 bypasses FUSE and leaves stale dentries, inode sizes
(i_size), and page-cache pages in the kernel VFS cache when physical
DUTs are reused across branches (e.g. M156/M157 vs ToT) or test suites
(e.g. ui_base_unittests vs unit_tests). On
android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests (builds #1944 and #1945), this
caused GlicApiTest, DevToolsExtensionTest,
PolicyPrefsTestCoverageTest.AllPoliciesHaveATestCase, and unit_tests
resource bundle loads to read stale or truncated cached files via
/sdcard even after PushChangedFiles extracted all 22,041 updated files
to /data/media/10.

Run 'sync && echo 3 > /proc/sys/vm/drop_caches' as root after pushing
test data in force_main_user mode so the /storage/emulated/<user> FUSE
mount re-fetches updated inodes and file contents from
/data/media/<user>.

parent
8cb0875453bf5d53d4ba26c4a48dc907a860198c [ diff ] 
[Ambient] Keep the chip when the bubble is closed

Previously, closing the ambient login bubble, e.g. with its close
button, dismissed the request, which also removed the "Sign in?" chip
from the location bar.

Now, closing the bubble keeps the request pending and the chip in the
location bar, so that the user can reopen the bubble from the chip. Like
a chip without a bubble, the chip then collapses, if it hasn't yet, and
expires, which ignores the request.

This adds PermissionRequest::ShouldKeepChipOnBubbleClose(), which
AmbientLoginPermissionRequest overrides. Closing the bubble still
dismisses other requests.

If the prompt is recreated, e.g. after switching tabs, the bubble opens
again. A follow-up will remember that the user closed the bubble, so
that only the chip is shown.

This also makes the accessors of PermissionPromptChipModel const.

Refactor Reporting API browser tests for consistency

Align `reporting_browsertest.cc` and `reporting_browsertest_android.cc`
on consistent conventions:
- Use `base::StrCat` instead of `base::StringPrintf` or `operator+` for
  constructing HTTP headers and test server URL paths.
- Assert that parsed report lists and looked-up dictionary fields are
  non-empty / non-null before dereferencing them.
- Standardize `EXPECT_EQ(actual, expected)` argument ordering and URL
  `.spec()` comparisons.
- Remove unused includes, helper methods, member variables, and
  redundant `SetUpOnMainThread()` overrides.

ea2b268538f0ff4357708d66184682167db3fb69 [ diff ] 
[rustmojo] Add Rust typemap for mojo_base.mojom.ReadOnlyFile

This allows us to parse Mojom `ReadOnlyFile` directly into a Rust
`File`.

In the process, we need to make sure that the file actually is
read-only. We need to do this both during parsing (for safety) and
during serialization (since if a user accidentally tries to send a
non-read-only file, we'll want to give a good error message
immediately).

As a side effect, we also add a `std::error::Error` impl for `MojoError`
because it _is_ an error, and this lets other code like `anyhow` use
error functionality directly.

[mojo] Add kMojoFixGeometricBufferGrowth2 feature

MojoMessage::AppendData accounts for 1.39% of browser UI-thread jank on
Windows Stable:
http://pprof/?id=d4bfdcce522614cc6b1eb08d23589447&pivot=mojo::core::ipcz_driver::MojoMessage::AppendData

crrev.com/c/7262407 added kMojoFixGeometricBufferGrowth to compute the
new buffer capacity from `data_storage_size_ * 2` instead of `data_size
* 2`, ensuring geometric capacity growth when
mojo::internal::Buffer::Allocate extends the message payload.

Because kMojoFixGeometricBufferGrowth is controlled by the TreesInViz
Finch study (which is not ready to launch to Stable yet) and Finch does
not support multiple studies controlling the same feature, this CL adds
kMojoFixGeometricBufferGrowth2 (disabled by default) with the same
effect so the optimization can be shipped independently.

Just moving MojoFixGeometricBufferGrowth from the TreesInViz study to a
new study wouldn't work well, as the feature would no longer be
guaranteed to be enabled in the "enabled" groups of TreesInViz.

Use MenuTestBase for MenuModelAdapterTest

MenuModelAdapterTest duplicated most of the setup that MenuTestBase
already provides (button, MenuRunner, Click(), SetUp/TearDown).
Rebase it on MenuTestBase and override BuildMenu() to make the
MenuModelAdapter the menu delegate so ui::MenuModel still receives
MenuWillShow()/MenuWillClose().

Add a protected button() accessor to MenuTestBase so subclasses can
click the anchor button to dismiss the menu.

🌺 [Android] Fix 2 extensions test setup and timing races on Brya desktop CI

Fix two extensions test setup and timing races observed in builds
#1943-#1945 of android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests:

1. WebRequestEventRouterContextDispatchTest (extensions_unittests):
   Initialize ScopedFeatureList in a base struct constructed before
   ExtensionsTest so FeatureList is initialized before
   BrowserTaskEnvironment spawns ThreadPool worker threads on Android
   (which query FeatureList::IsEnabled(kAndroidThreadPriority) during
   thread startup) and reset after ~ExtensionsTest() joins them.

2. InstanceIDApiTest.GetCreationTimeFormat (android_browsertests):
   Use >= instead of strict > when comparing creationTime against
   timeBeforeGetCreationTimeCall. On Android, InstanceIDBridge records
   creation time with integer millisecond resolution
   (System.currentTimeMillis()), so fast x86_64 desktop hardware can
   complete getID() within the same millisecond as Date.now().

tree
754b3b4b256faaa8af54971e3cdfcca3234911f5
parent
94a71461fe30b1c75e3140466415b44795f4a9ff [ diff ] 
[Layout] Position views before their children in BrowserViewLayout

This fixes a problem on macOS where the WebUI Toolbar fails to become
first responder when opening a new browser window.

The root cause is that NativeViewHost's bounds is set before its parent
view (https://crrev.com/c/7204789, Nov 2025). This hides NSView (as a
result of its visible bounds being 0x0 after clipping ancestors' bounds,
see logic in NativeViewHost::Layout), which casues AppKit to reject it
becoming the first responder.

This CL reverts https://crrev.com/c/7204789. This is now safe because
TopContainerView sets kViewDoesNotLayOutChildren since
crrev.com/c/7852780, so resizing it no longer lays out its children. Add
a DCHECK to enforce that views whose children are positioned by
BrowserViewLayout do not lay out their own children.

WebUIExtensionsContainer: Fix extension_popup_interactive_uitest.cc

Fix ExtensionPopupInteractiveUiTest.
APITriggeredPopupIsBlockedBySecurityDialog when the WebUI extensions
container is enabled. The test reached into the Views
ExtensionsToolbarDesktop via
ToolbarView::extensions_container_for_testing(), which is null with the
WebUI container, so the test crashed on a null dereference. Use the
platform-abstract ExtensionsContainer::From() instead, which resolves to
whichever container is active.

Also make
WebUIToolbarExtensionsContainer::ShowToolbarActionPopupForAPICall()
return false instead of CHECK-failing when the action no longer exists,
since the extension may be unloaded while the API call is in flight.

Tested with:
  interactive_ui_tests --gtest_filter=ExtensionPopupInteractiveUiTest.* \
    --enable-features=InitialWebUI,WebUIExtensionsContainer

tree
47087311559722f084c17f5ac008bff0f543c15a
parent
fbff40b173fab44a55950051b8d40fdf9b267926 [ diff ] 
[gfx] Avoid redundant DeriveFont and FontRenderParams lookups

During Views and BrowserView construction, ChromeTypographyProvider
repeatedly calls PlatformFont::GetFontSizeDeltaIgnoringUserOrLocaleSettings,
which invokes FontList().DeriveWithSizeDelta(size_delta) and constructs
a new PlatformFontSkia and queries GetFontRenderParams even when the
default font size matches kDefaultBaseFontSize (or when size_delta is 0).
Profiling linux-chromeos-rel browser_tests shows this accounts for
~0.5-0.7% of total CPU time (~20-30 ms CPU per test).

1. On non-Windows platforms where DeriveFont adjusts pixel size linearly
   by size_delta, return size_delta directly in
   GetFontSizeDeltaIgnoringUserOrLocaleSettings when the default font
   size equals kDefaultBaseFontSize.
2. In PlatformFontSkia::DeriveFont, return Font(this) when new_size,
   style, and weight are unchanged.

f5516af4e5922e902a7eedd104356a73d1b7adf1 [ diff ] 
[Prompt API] Let samplingMode take precedence over legacy params

When both samplingMode and legacy sampling parameters (temperature or
topK) are provided in contexts where AIPromptAPILegacyParams is enabled,
such as Chrome Extensions, let samplingMode take precedence and ignore
the legacy parameters after recording their deprecation warning instead
of throwing a TypeError. This matches the behavior on web pages and
allows callers to pass both during the migration period. Also switch
LanguageModel.params(), LanguageModel.topK, and
LanguageModel.temperature from MeasureAs to DeprecateAs in IDL.

[ntp-next] Tighten chip eligibility checks

The action chips element is only rendered by the page when NTP Next
features are enabled, so require that in both branches. Otherwise,
users with create image eligibility that are no fusebox eligible are
underreported in the AnyShown metric.

This does not mean people are getting chips that are not eligible
since the handler also checks for fusebox eligibility.

tree
5c098753e1904a3d32c9b26b637671123807520c
parent
0927e0d198866ce4d4903a76056f7813e16f3733 [ diff ] 
[updater] introduce shrink-to-tag-length and fix padding logic

- Fix `BinaryWriteTag` so `padded_length` acts as a minimum padded
  size for the tag space (pads with trailing zeros if the tag data is
  smaller, does not truncate if larger), rather than appending the
  entire length as additive padding.
- Update `tag_main.cc` handling of `--padded-length`:
  - If omitted, the tag space will be padded to the default minimum
    size (`kDefaultBinaryTagBytes` = 8206 bytes), regardless of
    whether an empty or populated tag is provided. Note: on Windows,
    `sign.py` calls `tag.exe --set-tag` with no `--padded-length`.
    With this change, the tag area in the official Windows
    metainstaller drops from 8220 to 8206 bytes, matching the
    8192-character tag limit.
  - Passing `--padded-length=0` shrinks the tag space to the minimum
    length required to hold the tag data.
  - Passing `--padded-length > 0` pads the tag space to at least that
    specified minimum size.
- In `chrome/updater/tools/BUILD.gn`, the copied binary name is
  updated so it is named `tag` instead of `tag.exe` on non-Windows
  platforms.
- Add and update test PKG fixtures and unit tests in
  `tag_unittest.cc` to verify accurate size expectations, custom
  padding, untruncated tags exceeding padded length, and better
  coverage of tag injection logic.

[LHS] Pass color scheme and application locale to NTP LHS URLs

Supports dark/light mode and browser language/direction (LTR/RTL) for
the NTP Left-Hand Side (LHS) rail:

1. In `NewTabPageUI::GetLoadTimeData`, append the color scheme (`cs=0|1`
via `lens::AppendDarkModeParamToURL` and `csui=3`) and application
locale (`hl=<locale>`) query parameters to `expandedLhsUrl`.

2. Pass `this.theme_?.isDark` from `ntp-app` to `cr-left-hand-side` and
dynamically update the `#expanded` `<ntp-iframe>` `src` URL's `cs` and
`csui=3` parameters when the NTP theme changes between light and dark
mode.

3. Add unit tests in `new_tab_page_ui_unittest.cc`,
`left_hand_side_test.ts`, and `app_test.ts`.

occasional server failure):

8224f972c2eb4f03bf119e64c663cdc93f1f2268 [ diff ] 
[EdgeToEdge] Offset Hub and TabListEditor at top

When top window insets are consumed for the top scalp, the root
coordinator view extends under the status bar. While browsing UI is
offset by TopControlsStacker via the TOP_SCALP layer, fullscreen
overlays attached directly to the root coordinator bypass
TopControlsStacker and draw under the status bar. This CL updates
HubManagerImpl and TabListEditorMediator to register an
EdgeToEdgePadAdjuster with EdgeToEdgeController and apply a top margin
equal to the system top inset whenever top insets are consumed:

- HubManagerImpl sets topMargin on HubContainerView, offsetting the Hub
  toolbar, pane host, snackbar container, and hub_overlay_container
  (including TabGridDialog and TabListEditor when shown inside Hub).
- TabListEditorMediator sets TOP_MARGIN on TabListEditorLayout when
  hosted outside of an already-offset Hub container (such as when opened
  from the bottom tab-group strip into R.id.coordinator).

before: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTE1NDgzMjY4Mjg0NDE2MHxkYjFlZWUzOC1hNQ
after: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjU5MTk3MjIyNjYyOTYzMnw2NTJhODYxMS0zYQ

[Actor] Register AttemptLoginToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest subclasses
expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and ToolRegistry
discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

This CL registers AttemptLoginToolRequest in ToolRegistry by adding
ToolId::kAttemptLogin, declaring kModelFacingName ("attempt_login"), and
implementing GetToolDefinition() with an empty parameter schema (login
targets are optional in backend execution). It also adds unit test
coverage in tool_registry_unittest.cc.

[Actor] Register LoadAndExtractContentToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest subclasses
expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and ToolRegistry
discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

This CL registers LoadAndExtractContentToolRequest in ToolRegistry by
adding ToolId::kLoadAndExtractContent, declaring kModelFacingName
("load_and_extract_content"), and implementing GetToolDefinition() with
required urls array parameter. It also adds unit test coverage in
tool_registry_unittest.cc under desktop build guards.

🌺 [Android] Reset deferred_startup_complete_ in PersistedTabDataAndroidBrowserTest

In PersistedTabDataAndroidBrowserTest.TestDeferredStartup
(android_browsertests), ProcessInitializationHandler's idle handler can
fire OnDeferredStartup() during SetUpOnMainThread() on fast x86_64
desktop hardware (android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests), setting
PersistedTabDataAndroid::deferred_startup_complete_ to true before the
test queues its deferred requests.

Reset PersistedTabDataAndroid::deferred_startup_complete_ to false at
the start of TestDeferredStartup so the deferred request queue is
exercised deterministically before OnDeferredStartup() is called.

[prefs] Avoid duplicate DefaultPrefStore lookups on PrefService reads
📋 Metadata
commit
1323b322d6562733f8bae187a06b06a1f1aeb643
[ log ]
author
Erik Chen <erikchen@chromium.org>
Sat Oct 17 01:21:18 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 17 01:21:18 2026
tree
2fdf83faf2377a15c5e5f5c3fe2a89d3b380331a
parent
0a3ed81a508b3085d85318469e15571062048de4 [ diff ] 
[prefs] Avoid duplicate DefaultPrefStore lookups on PrefService reads

Every PrefService::GetValue() / GetBoolean() / GetInteger() / etc. call
invokes PrefService::GetPreferenceValue(path), which first calls
pref_registry_->defaults()->GetValue(path, &default_value) (searching a
~2,500-entry std::map in DefaultPrefStore) to verify registration and
obtain default_type = default_value->type(). Previously, it discarded
the retrieved default_value pointer and called
pref_value_store_->GetValue(), which for the >95% of preferences not
overridden in non-default stores walked down to DEFAULT_STORE (the exact
same pref_registry_->defaults() DefaultPrefStore instance) and searched
its std::map a second time.

This CL:
1. Adds PrefValueStore::GetValueFromNonDefaultStores() and uses it in
   PrefService::GetPreferenceValue(), returning the default_value
   pointer already retrieved by the initial registration lookup when no
   non-default store overrides the pref.
2. Calls PrefValueStore::GetValue() directly in
   PrefService::Preference::GetValue(), since Preference objects are
   only constructed after FindPreference() verifies registration in
   pref_registry_->defaults() and caches the expected type_.

🌺 [Android] Fix 2 Java test setup and timing races on Brya desktop CI

Fix two Java test setup and timing races observed in builds #1943-#1945
of android-desktop-17-x64-rel-brya-tests:

1. TabAlertIndicatorTest.testMediaStateHistogram
   (chrome_public_test_apk):
   Call exitPictureInPicture() and waitForMediaState(mTab,
   MediaState.NONE) before ending testMediaStateHistogram() (matching
   testAlertStatePipPlaying and testAlertStatePriority) so leaving PiP
   active does not trigger an asynchronous Activity relaunch that
   destroys the shared WebContents during the next batched test.

2. BottomSheetObserverTest.setUp (chrome_public_unit_test_apk):
   Wait for mBottomSheetController.getContainerHeight() > 0 in setUp()
   before running test bodies that call setSheetOffsetFromBottom().

[Cast Streaming] Tune the mirroring video encoder for screen content
📋 Metadata
commit
31d206393f8a680c5d53b24f8b7cbdf7e356bb9e
[ log ]
author

committer

Sat Oct 17 01:17:22 2026
tree
ec6070508573ff7e305a234cf8e6b69b405f4920
parent
7319966f146d6f1c75e6d356f0d8a2f76f045072 [ diff ] 
[Cast Streaming] Tune the mirroring video encoder for screen content

MediaVideoEncoderWrapper never set media::VideoEncoder::Options's
content_hint, so mirroring sessions used camera tuning. The legacy
media/cast VpxEncoder, removed in crrev.com/c/7833607, always enabled
VP8E_SET_SCREEN_CONTENT_MODE (VP8) or VP9E_CONTENT_SCREEN (VP9) along
with a higher static threshold. Losing that tuning contributes to soft,
smeared text when casting text-heavy tabs such as Google Docs.

This CL sets content_hint to ContentHint::Screen. For software encoding
this restores screen content mode and the higher static threshold. For
hardware encoding, VideoEncodeAcceleratorAdapter maps it to
VideoEncodeAccelerator::Config::ContentType::kDisplay. As with WebRTC
screen sharing, this also raises libvpx VP8's minimum quantizer to 12.

This is part 1 of a group of fixes for poor text quality and low
bitrate in Cast mirroring:
  1. Chromium: Tune the encoder for screen content (this CL).
     https://crrev.com/c/8578554
  2. Chromium: Raise the default max video bitrate to 10 Mbps.
     https://crrev.com/c/8576027
  3. Open Screen: Make BandwidthEstimator byte based.
     https://crrev.com/c/8583477
  4. Chromium: Only reduce mirroring bandwidth on real congestion
     signals.
     https://crrev.com/c/8578555

Bug: 572902679
Test: cast_unittests --gtest_filter=MediaVideoEncoderWrapperTest.*

transitive dependencies) from the sysroot package lists. Rebuild and
upload all sysroots so they no longer include those packages.

[TabUI Virtualization] Extract item helpers in TabListDataProvider

Extract protected insertItem(TabModel, TabListItem) and
updateItemAt(int, TabListItem, @PayloadType int) helpers in
TabListDataProvider, and update getInsertionIndex to accept a
TabListItem instead of a tab ID. This allows GroupedTabListDataProvider
to reuse the same insertion and in-place update helpers for TabGroupItem
entries in follow-up CLs.

[TTC] Enable kSwitchTab on Android

Previously, SwitchTabTool and the FindMatchingTabs() /
ResolveTabInWindow() helpers in page_search_utils were desktop-only
because they iterated TabStripModel and looked up windows via
BrowserWindowInterface::FromSessionID(), neither of which exists on
Android. As a result, TTC could not register or dispatch
actor::ToolId::kSwitchTab there.

This change ports page_search_utils to TabListInterface and
SwitchTabTool to Tool::FindBrowserWindow(), moves switch_tab_tool and
switch_tab_tool_request out of skip_android_unmigrated_actor_files, and
enables kSwitchTab in ttc::ToolController on Android.

switch_tab_tool_browsertest.cc is ported to TabListInterface so it runs
in android_browsertests. page_search_utils_unittest.cc now registers a
TabListBridge on its mock window, since FindMatchingTabs() resolves the
tab list through TabListInterface::From() rather than
GetTabStripModel().

NO_IFTTT=Only moving an existing ToolRequest type out of SKIP_ANDROID_UNMIGRATED_ACTOR_FILES guard

Bug: 470475787, 473667354
Test: browser_tests, android_browsertests, unit_tests

[TTC] Enable kCloseTab on Android

Now that TabManagementTool builds on Android, enable
actor::ToolId::kCloseTab and ToolController::CloseCurrentTab in
ttc::ToolController on Android.

This also ports tab_management_tool_browsertest.cc from desktop-only
TabStripModel and ui_test_utils APIs to cross-platform TabListInterface
and BrowserWindowInterface so it runs in android_browsertests, and adds
unit test coverage for the CreateTab, ActivateTab, and CloseTab
ToolRequests in actor_proto_conversion_unittest.cc and for CloseTab in
event_dispatcher_unittest.cc. The cross-profile incognito window tests
remain desktop-only because creating an incognito window on Android
launches a new Activity.

Manually tested kCloseTab via TTC on an Android desktop device.

Android: Fix O(N^2) duplicate check in zip_helpers.add_to_zip_hermetic

Checking `zip_path not in zip_file.namelist()` allocated and scanned a
new list of all existing entry names on every call. Using the dict
`zip_file.NameToInfo` makes each check O(1).

For `dex.py` on `chrome_java` (3,857 zip entries), this reduces clean
dex time from 2,131 ms to 1,943 ms (-188 ms, 9% faster) and 1-file
incremental dex time from 701 ms to 518 ms (-183 ms, 26% faster).

Bug: 574547715

[Omnibox] Replace disabled HTTPS location bar color tests with unit test

testHttpsLocationBarColor (crbug.com/556414361) and
testHttpsLocationBarColor_PageInfoAsAppMenuItemFlagEnabled
(crbug.com/556408574) were disabled because the CQ AVD emulator image
never delivers page theme color from theme_color_test.html to the tab.

HTTPS security icon visibility with and without
ANDROID_PAGE_INFO_AS_APP_MENU_ITEM is already covered by
OmniboxTest#testSecurityIconOnHTTPS,
OmniboxTest#testSecurityIconOnHTTPS_PageInfoAsAppMenuItemFlagEnabled,
and StatusMediatorUnitTest. Add a Robolectric unit test in
LocationBarModelUnitTest for shouldEmphasizeHttpsScheme() and
isUsingBrandColor() across default theme color, custom brand color,
native page, and incognito tab states, and remove the disabled
instrumentation tests.

7d445623a2478e9bb3f9924ac49e3830529330f8
parent
98f84cfbcbc6718d0b0b186956748be1da6a691b [ diff ] 
[Win] Use hardware rendering for UI elements

Several browser UI widgets force software compositing even when the main
browser window is GPU-composited.

These widgets cause D3D10Warp.dll to be loaded and for a Warp D3D device
to be created, impacting GPU process reference set. On the DWM side, the
software DXGI swapchain must be copied to shadow D3D texturew before
rendering it to the desktop, impacting its commit size.

Remove these overrides for menus, tooltips, status bubbles, omnibox
popups and the eyedropper. These widgets can now use GPU compositing
when available and still switch to software through the normal global
fallback.

Keep non-Windows behavior unchanged and remove the unused omnibox
software-compositing kill switch.

Code comments mention waiting until http://crbug.com/40198772 being
fixed first. It already has.

Bug: 40198772

Android: Preserve status bar insets when window loses focus

On Samsung One UI (Android 14), launching the system share sheet
(`ChooserActivity`) overlays a translucent activity that takes window
focus and causes `WindowManager` to hide `Type.statusBars()`. This
dispatches `onApplyWindowInsets` to Chrome's unfocused window with
`statusBars` visible insets set to `0` while
`getInsetsIgnoringVisibility(Type.statusBars())` remains `77px`,
removing `EdgeToEdgeBaseLayout`'s top padding and shifting
`CompositorViewHolder` upward by `77px` while Chrome remains visible
underneath.

Preserve `Type.statusBars()` in `InsetObserver` from
`getInsetsIgnoringVisibility(Type.statusBars())` when the window is
unfocused and status bars were visible when focused on Android R+. Gated
behind the enabled-by-default CachedFlag
kAndroidPreserveStatusBarInsetsWhenUnfocused so it can be turned off via
Finch.

Before/After (Samsung Galaxy A52 5G, Android 14, One UI 6.1, bottom bar
enabled; tapping Share in the omnibox twice):

- Before:
  https://drive.google.com/file/d/1wDQH3M8uLgr9uS0Ee_UqZ4_HnT68wCFB/view
- After:
  https://drive.google.com/file/d/1RZbJgJJYFWQ67c27qfbXBmWme514xNBK/view
- Side-by-side:
  https://drive.google.com/file/d/1lBbrDep2NLykLGplwJxWJYEP4d2iKvdT/view

Fixed: 519939590

[glic] Add GlicFreImpressionCuiTracker for FRE impression CUI.

Unconsented Glic opens lack a dedicated CUI tracker measuring the time
and outcome from panel invocation until the First Run Experience (FRE)
opt-in prompt renders.

Add GlicFreImpressionCuiTracker to record
Glic.CUI.FreImpression.{Outcome,Latency} starting in OnOpen() when
!GlicEnabling::HasConsentedForProfile(profile_). Resolve as kSuccess on
kOptInImpression (including background-warmed instances where
MaybeRecordOptInImpression() ran in OnVisibilityChanged(true) before
OnOpen()), kUnknownCancel without Latency if kUserInputSubmitted fires
first in Message-First FRE, kAbandoned on close/hide, and kFailed on
WebUI errors.

[BottomBar] Update flag configs for AndroidBottomBar experiment

Evaluating the AndroidBottomBar experiment on device requires tuning
browser controls lockout delays to prevent toolbar churn during page
load and transient reveals. Additionally, the Glic setting toggle was
previously defaulted to false during scaffolding, preventing
Glic-eligible participants from configuring the bottom bar extra action
toggle without server-side flag overrides. Defaulting it to true makes
the setting available to eligible users while preserving existing
downstream Glic eligibility checks.

Fixed: 574523924

settings: Enable SettingsInTab in browserservices Java tests

Remove @DisableFeatures(SETTINGS_IN_TAB, SETTINGS_IN_TAB_DESKTOP) from
the two Trusted Web Activity tests that still opted out:

- ManageTrustedWebActivityDataActivityTest polled for a running
  SettingsActivity after launching the WebAPK site settings intent. With
  SettingsInTab enabled on tablets and desktop, site settings open in a
  browser tab instead, so the test now accepts either a SettingsActivity
  or an activity whose SettingsHostFragment is showing
  SingleWebsiteSettings, depending on
  SettingsInTab.shouldOpenSettingsInTab().

- TrustedWebActivityPreferencesUiTest launches settings through
  SiteSettingsTestUtils, which already forces SettingsActivity via
  setUseSettingsActivityForTesting(), so the annotation was redundant.

Bug: 521895796, 556881398
Test: ManageTrustedWebActivityDataActivityTest, TrustedWebActivityPreferencesUiTest

tree
1ccc203da45439a02f592c017f71fba85728a7f9
parent
0298a7770c66d854a9d11335682b9c434805d775 [ diff ] 
updater: avoid user32 when looking up explorer.exe processes

IsExplorerRunningAtMediumOrLower() and GetExplorerPidInActiveSession()
use base::NamedProcessIterator to find the explorer.exe processes.
NamedProcessIterator compares executable names with
base::FilePath::CompareEqualIgnoreCase(), which calls ::CharUpperW() in
user32 and CHECKs base::win::IsUser32AndGdi32Available(). That check
returns false whenever the OS reports the win32k syscall-disable
mitigation for the process.

The updater never applies this mitigation, but on hosts that
virtualize win32k, such as GraphOn GO-Global, updater processes
launched from a hosted application (Chrome's periodic
`updater.exe --wake-all`) report the mitigation as enabled, and every
such process crashes in GetUACState() during startup.

Replace NamedProcessIterator with base::ProcessIterator and a filter
that uses an ASCII case-insensitive comparison, which has no user32
dependency. The process name is pure ASCII, so this is equivalent.

Add a unit test that exercises GetUACState().

Bug: 574549798

tree
bcebf36d603410781ce763eed05b89b2b1ce121c

c30865021cd1476643b454faea0941c2f7838123 [ diff ] 
Check transcript exclusion before starting speech recognition

Check GlicMediaContext::IsExcludedFromTranscript() in
CaptionListenerImpl::IsTranscriptionAllowed() so frames with active
WebRTC peer connections or user media capture do not start speech
recognition and create unnecessary SODA instances. Previously, these
frames could still start recognition and only drop transcripts in
GlicMediaContext::OnResult().

Also notify SpeechRecognitionClientBrowserInterface when WebRTC peer
connections are added or removed. This updates speech recognition
availability for observers in affected frames, resetting active
recognizers when a peer connection starts and allowing transcription to
resume once all peer connections are removed.

Bug: 568545566

[fuchsia][starview] Add sandbox, mojo, and ipc test filter files

This change adds test filter files to disable failing test cases
observed on fuchsia-starview-qemu-tests for sandbox_linux_unittests,
mojo_unittests, and ipc_tests.

Note: These filters track features and platform capabilities currently
unsupported in the Cuttlefish guest image. No support or action is
expected from the Chrome or WebView on Android teams.

44b0d4e8b30f51f51ca1d59617def71fa3b84206 [ diff ] 
Move payment_handler_header_view_ui_browsertest to payments

Moves payment_handler_header_view_ui_browsertest.cc out of the
monolithic //chrome/test:browser_tests target into
//chrome/browser/ui/views/payments:browser_tests.

Although the entry in //chrome/test:browser_tests was guarded by
`if (!is_android || enable_vr)` with a TODO noting that guard, the
entire //chrome/test:browser_tests target is already inside an outer
`if (!is_android)` block (line 1326). As a result, `!is_android ||
enable_vr` is tautologically true inside `browser_tests`, and moving
the file to //chrome/browser/ui/views/payments:browser_tests (which is
unconditionally linked into `browser_tests` on `!is_android` and
already depends on `:test_support` and
`//components/payments/content/icon`) preserves the exact same build
conditions.

[Omnibox] Re-enable UrlBarUiTest#testAutocorrectionChangesTriggerCorrectSuggestions

testAutocorrectionChangesTriggerCorrectSuggestions was disabled on
Desktop (crbug.com/477262537) when UrlBarTest ran inside a full
ChromeTabbedActivity whose background AutocompleteController
asynchronously overwrote inline autocomplete spans.

Since crrev.com/c/8496944 migrated UrlBarTest to UrlBarUiTest running in
BlankUiTestActivity with a mocked UrlBarDelegate, there is no background
AutocompleteController and its sibling test
testAutocompletionChangesTriggerCorrectSuggestions runs cleanly on
Desktop.

c02f9fc984eae63c9712a74025fb58ae4b9c8720 [ diff ] 
Support tab state persistence and restore for single-model windows

Update tab state loading, restoration, and model tracking to handle
single-profile windows where the unsupported model is a StubTabModel
(TabModelType.EMPTY) under EnforceIncognitoIsolation:

- In ChromeTabbedActivity, ignore Incognito state files in REGULAR
  windows and Regular state files in OFF_THE_RECORD windows when
  sEnforceIncognitoIsolation is enabled.
- In TabStateStore, handle ignoring regular files or both regular and
  incognito files in loadState().
- In CombinedTabRestorer, start the incognito restorer when the regular
  restorer is null, and call onRestoreFinished() when both are null.
- In ModelTrackingOrchestrator, skip creating the regular synchronizer
  when the regular TabModel has TabModelType.EMPTY.
- Skip creating the archived tab model and acquiring BackgroundTabPool
  when the regular TabModel has TabModelType.EMPTY.

tree
1fdde2726e899ea852a4c132eaa0e87e4a446442
parent
c1aea88cb405d84fd3e83b482940455c204e70fb [ diff ] 
[UnboundedElement] Let privileged subframes escape

Privileged extension subframes (such as the Contextual Tasks tab picker)
are embedded inside Google Search host frames where
`GetOutermostMainFrame()` does not have `kAllowedPrivileged`. Requiring
both the subframe and `GetOutermostMainFrame()` to be privileged in
`GetUnboundedElementConstrainingBoundsInScreen` clamped privileged
extension menus to the side panel viewport.

Because `web_accessible_resources` only checks the immediate initiator
origin rather than the full ancestor chain, add
`ContentBrowserClient::IsUnboundedElementEscapingAllowed` so the
embedder verifies both that the subframe is an allowlisted extension and
that `GetOutermostMainFrame()` has a Google origin before allowing the
element to escape the root view.

[glic] Remove unused kGlicReloadMaxLoadingTimeMs

kGlicReloadMaxLoadingTimeMs ("glic-reload-max-loading-time-ms") and the
corresponding "reloadMaxLoadingTimeMs" loadTimeData value in GlicUI were
originally used by FreAppController for Glic FRE reloads. Since the FRE
WebUI and FreAppController were removed in https://crrev.com/c/7899786,
this parameter is no longer read anywhere.

04fd5517eabcb6a593079f68de7f9e687d292640
parent
f0376f3033bee21497cb57d838c20a1e4b4320ce [ diff ] 
[Cast] Fix false in-flight frame drops and rejected frame state

Fixes two issues in media/cast/sender that caused cascading frame drops
after static screen intervals:

1. OpenscreenFrameSender::EnqueueFrame previously updated
last_enqueued_frame_id_, last_send_time_, send_target_playout_delay_,
and frame_rtp_timestamps_ before calling sender_->EnqueueFrame(). When a
frame was dropped for an invalid reference or rejected by OpenScreen
(e.g., MAX_DURATION_IN_FLIGHT), GetInFlightMediaDuration() queried the
rejected frame's RTP timestamp, inflating the in-flight duration and
dropping subsequent frames. State is now updated only after
sender_->EnqueueFrame() succeeds.

2. VideoSender::InsertRawVideoFrame and OnEncodedVideoFrame previously
used the raw inter-frame reference_time delta when frames_in_encoder_
> 0, counting idle capture gaps (50ms static screen refreshes or 0Hz
capture pauses) as media backlog and causing spurious
kInFlightDurationTooHigh drops. Per-frame duration in the encoder is now
capped at 1 / MaxFrameRate().

Verification:
- Repro (Before): out/Default/cast_unittests --gtest_filter="OpenscreenFrameSenderTest.RejectedFrameDoesNotCorruptState:VideoSenderTest.DoesNotDropFrameAfterStaticScreenGapWithFrameInEncoder" -> 2 tests failed
- Fix (After): out/Default/cast_unittests --gtest_filter="OpenscreenFrameSenderTest.RejectedFrameDoesNotCorruptState:VideoSenderTest.DoesNotDropFrameAfterStaticScreenGapWithFrameInEncoder" -> 2 tests passed
- Regression: out/Default/cast_unittests -> 161 passed, 0 failures

TAG=agy
CONV=ef7ed781-f039-437d-a35a-19f92c59193f

080ca3a5a8d4f43d3eaec7fcc74b376101f98763 [ diff ] 
[glic-si] Remove legacy glic window geometry metrics

The glic window geometry metrics were driven by the single-instance
glic window and have no callers in multi-instance glic; the floating UI
reports resizes to GlicInstanceMetrics instead. Remove them together
with the panel position, size, presentation-time and resize metrics they
recorded, the GlicMetrics::Delegate abstraction that only they (and the
response path removed earlier) used, and the disabled interactive test
for the position metrics.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Only recorded by the single-instance glic window, which no longer exists; see Glic.Instance.PanelPresentationTime.* and Glic.Instance.Floaty.UserResizeEnded.* for multi-instance equivalents.

4731f06794ecdde95ced92337e044c4859c0ba64 [ diff ] 
[glic-si] Remove GlicMetrics::OnResponseStarted() and tests

GlicMetrics::OnResponseStarted() is never called in production since
instance_metrics_backwards_compatibility() returns GlicInstanceMetrics
now. The part of GlicMetrics::OnUserInputSubmitted() after the
kGlicFixTimeToFirstQueryKillSwitch check never runs either, because
its only caller invokes it only when the kill switch is enabled.

Remove OnResponseStarted(), the unreachable part of
OnUserInputSubmitted() (and its now-unused parameters), the turn state
they shared, the browser activity observer, and the session aggregates
fed only by this path (response count and input modes used). The
Glic.WindowOpen UKM now uses ukm::NoURLSourceId() explicitly, which is
what it always recorded in practice.

Also remove OnResponseStarted() from
GlicInstanceMetricsBackwardsCompatibility. GlicWebClientHandler now
calls it on GlicInstanceMetrics directly through
Host::instance_metrics().

Also remove kGlicFixTimeToFirstQueryKillSwitch, which was landed as
enabled by default in March https://crrev.com/c/7675059.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Only recorded by the single-instance GlicMetrics response path, which is unreachable in multi-instance glic; superseded by Glic.Turn.* and Glic.Instance.* metrics in GlicInstanceMetrics.

6debcb163ed2ed08be479c845abad16c581b9a02
parent
efb0817b39eca8ba984c3ecb8121ab79469810b8 [ diff ] 
[Android Test Runner] Disable system animations for gtest suites

Android instrumentation test suites disable system animations via
TestAnimations.java, whereas native gtest suites (android_browsertests,
content_browsertests, etc.) leave system animations at 1.0x. Because
browser tests execute one test per am instrument invocation and
BrowserTestBase::TearDown() waits for HAS_DESTROYED_ACTIVITIES, every
test animates full window open and close transitions back to the
launcher (~1.0-1.3s per test, or ~12-13% of android_browsertests bot
time).

d37aac9ce04e436242c55cb8492a391f9f2bc9e8 [ diff ] 
[viz] Record actual frame intervals for more decided rates

Viz.FrameIntervalDecider.ActualIntervalFor{DecidedHz}hz records the
actual BeginFrame interval while a decided rate is applied, but only
for 20, 24, 25 and 30 Hz. Add 10, 12, 15 and 60 Hz, which are common
results for animated images, stepped animations and video, e.g. from
MixedFixedIntervalMatcher on Android.

Move the recording to FrameIntervalDecider::RecordActualFrameInterval()
and drive it from a table, so that it can be unit tested.

Bug: 555345506
Test: viz_unittests --gtest_filter=FrameIntervalDeciderActualInterval*

> Fix cr-action-menu positioning for unbounded elements
>

> - Allows escaping for unbounded elements based on the frame's authorization without requiring the outermost main frame to be privileged.

[remoting] Add AgentIntegrationConfigurator and Linux implementation

Implements the AgentIntegrationConfigurator interface and its Linux
implementation, which checks for the presence of the
"chrome-remote-desktop-agent-integration" binary in the host executable
directory and invokes it with "status", "enable", or "disable" on a
background task runner. Also adds FakeAgentIntegrationConfigurator,
MockAgentIntegrationConfigurator, and unit tests.

parent
ed2084d6f5654717c91144a79584d2afcadaa3d2 [ diff ] 
Log whether the risk-based preflight wait timed out

When card-on-device verification is enforced, the risk-based
authentication of a masked server card waits for the preflight call
response for up to 3 seconds. How long the wait took is recorded in
Autofill.BetterAuth.UserPerceivedLatencyOnCardSelection.OptedIn.Duration,
but whether it timed out was only recorded for the flows that fall back
to CVC authentication, in .OptedIn.TimedOutCvcFallback. That histogram
is not logged for the risk-based flow, because a timeout there starts
the risk-based authentication without the response instead of falling
back to CVC. Record that outcome for the risk-based flow in the new
.RiskBased.TimedOut histogram.

[Omnibox] Replace disabled OmniboxPedalsTest with Robolectric unit tests

OmniboxPedalsTest was disabled on Desktop (crbug.com/481445129) and had
testPedalsStartedOnTabEnterKeyStroke disabled for flakiness
(crbug.com/495701957) due to full-browser activity startup and focus/IME
timing dependencies.

Individual pedal execution paths are already covered in
OmniboxPedalUnitTest, OmniboxActionDelegateImplUnitTest, and
AutocompleteMediatorUnitTest. Add Robolectric unit test coverage for
OmniboxPedal chip model population and click handling in
ActionChipsProcessorUnitTest and Tab+Enter action chip activation in
BaseSuggestionViewUnitTest, and remove the redundant instrumentation test
suite.

7388ae2db0ff8305a433b234bdf4fa372707d982 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Disallow error pages from allowlisting DNR frames

When a navigation fails and commits an error page,
RenderFrameHost::GetLastCommittedURL() retains the failed destination
URL rather than an error URL. Cross-document URL spoofing on error
commits was addressed separately in crbug.com/511736672; this CL handles
the genuine failed URL.

Previously, Declarative Net Request evaluated allowAllRequests rules
against error-page navigations, allowing an error document whose failed
URL matched an allowAllRequests rule to poison allowlisted_frames_ and
exempt its subresource loads from DNR rules.

Skip evaluating allowAllRequests rules for a frame's own navigation when
committing or same-document navigating within an error document, while
still updating allowlisted_frames_ to evict any stale entry from a prior
document in the same RenderFrameHost and preserve any allowlist
inherited from an ancestor frame.

Fixed: 519999855

tree
456174af9d4622fdcf68a2929daf9edc10baf3dc
parent
cecf5eb3d95e234783d43ce2a12274511d2e349e [ diff ] 
[Unified tabstrip] Update drag bounds calculations.

Update GetSourceViewOffsetFromMouse to use GetTabStripViewTargetBounds
(and the group header's target bounds for Type::GROUP nodes) so drag
mouse offsets are calculated against target layout bounds instead of
preferred sizes (needed for horizontal dynamic tab sizing) or stale
mid-animation group bounds, or live bounds before drag starts. Also
updates TabDragHandlerImpl to use the group header view instead of the
group view as the dragged view for group header drags. Also updates
TabView::OnMousePressed to convert the mouse event into the root view
since active tab changes from mouse press can cause bounds changes (this
was handled similarly in the legacy horizontal tab strip).

parent
04cfbcfa6ac28f070c5662414efd3a194ac9a535 [ diff ] 
[Android] Resolve picker file paths before forwarding to native

SelectFileDialog forwarded picker-supplied file: paths to native as-is
after a canonical-path check. Since native re-opens the path later, the
file that was checked and the file that is opened could differ.
Canonicalize the path once, validate the result, and forward that
instead.

GetDisplayNameTask now also rejects file:// paths that are not existing
regular files, consistent with FilePathSelectedTask. Tests updated to
use real files.

146cb891d49af5b09baf2d828e05f784a392321c [ diff ] 
[a11y] Remove title parameter from TextAlternativeFromTitleAttribute

Follow-up to a review nit on https://crrev.com/c/8551193.

AXNodeObject::TextAlternativeFromTooltip() is the only caller of
AXNodeObject::TextAlternativeFromTitleAttribute(), and it only reads
kTitleAttr to pass the value in.

Move the FastGetAttribute(kTitleAttr) lookup into
TextAlternativeFromTitleAttribute() and remove the title parameter,
matching AXNodeObject::Description().

I've omitted the Bug footer so this cleanup is not tracked for branch
merges.

6e8bd182b2bda997a15f25abf68d8a61022490f2
parent
3c93f10e2672d356f6070e0d18cc05848ea00163 [ diff ] 
[UIA] Preserve valid UIA offsets in named empty text fields

An empty non-atomic text field can return itself as its empty-object
ancestor. Relocating positive offsets to that same anchor snaps valid
interior UIA name offsets to the end. Validation during GetText can
therefore undo backward character movement.

Relocate only genuine descendants and let same-anchor positions use
normal offset clamping.

Release accessibility contexts when disabling an inactive agent
📋 Metadata
commit
cd4e07730fd8c2f85e5740b21e33047a8f2fce20
[ log ]
author
Alexander Semashko <ahest@yandex-team.ru>
Fri Oct 16 23:19:29 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 16 23:19:29 2026
tree
0581fabc877c2f9059e3c8aa0df261b75d729066
parent
ff081c61d285c2b1aa77f94dcc652f929b532434 [ diff ] 
Release accessibility contexts when disabling an inactive agent

Accessibility.getFullAXTree can create an AXContext without enabling the
Accessibility agent. During session teardown, Dispose() calls disable(),
but its enabled-state early return leaves the context in the agent's
GC-managed document_to_context_map_.

The context is then released by GC instead of during teardown. Removing
the last context explicitly disposes the document's AXObjectCache, so
delayed finalization can invalidate AX objects during another
allocation.

Always release inspector registrations, contexts, and cached node state
in disable(). Keep EnabledAgents cleanup conditional on the previous
enabled state.

Add a unit test for getFullAXTree followed by Dispose without enable.

5a07708ed8425fe3f202589aa87309ec40ec8883 [ diff ] 
[Cast] Record Cast Streaming trace categories in media-router-internals

The "Start tracing" button in chrome://media-router-internals recorded
"media.cast", which no trace event uses, so none of the Cast Streaming
events in media/cast (e.g. frame drops, encoding, and transport) were
captured. It also recorded "base" and "toplevel", which are so
high-volume that, with "record-until-full", the buffer filled in about
20 seconds. A trace attached to a recent customer report covered only
20.8 seconds and lacked the frame drop reasons needed to diagnose it.

Record "cast.stream" and "cast_perf_test" instead of "media.cast", and
drop "base" and "toplevel" so that traces cover longer sessions.

tree
59a183cdc3ac73660b31877ba2ea3cb16c7df307
parent
dcf41093b1a52fca7cfcc6b6a1bb83bd36b34d53 [ diff ] 
Fix SharedTabGroupInteractiveUiTest startup crash and screenshot skip

SharedTabGroupInteractiveUiTest tests call CreateNewTabGroup() followed
immediately by ShareTabGroup() at the start of the test body.
TabGroupSyncServiceImpl initializes asynchronously on startup; when
AddToNewGroup() triggers TabGroupSyncServiceImpl::AddGroup() before
initialization completes, AddGroup() defers adding the group into
pending_actions_ rather than inserting it into SavedTabGroupModel. The
subsequent ShareTabGroup() call invokes
SavedTabGroupModel::MakeTabGroupSharedForTesting(), where
GetMutableGroup(local_group_id) returns nullptr and crashes with
EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION.

Wait for TabGroupSyncServiceInitializedObserver in
SharedTabGroupInteractiveUiTest::SetUpOnMainThread() so the service is
initialized before tests create and share tab groups. Also use
OnIncompatibleAction::kIgnoreAndContinue instead of kSkipTest before
Screenshot() in SharedTabGroupInAppMenu, SharedTabGroupInEverythingMenu,
and SharedTabGroupInTabStrip so post-screenshot menu cleanup steps still
run on non-pixel builders and retries are not marked as skipped.

tree
765e1c808ef58fa32524553644b96cf34e62f7f5
parent
15631dd1ce0d5edaefe91701370e7bebc956cb4d [ diff ] 
Fix SolidColorAnalyzer ignoring difference RRect clips

On MacOS, BubbleBorder::DIALOG_SHADOW is NO_SHADOW, so
BubbleBorder::PaintNoShadow clears the bubble corners in the widget's
root layer with clipRRect(kDifference) + drawColor(transparent, kSrc).
SolidColorAnalyzer only checked whether a kClipRRect covered the tile
and ignored the clip op, so for tiles inside the bubble it treated the
difference clip as a no-op, saw the clear, and reported the tile as
solid transparent. cc then drew nothing for those tiles, leaving a
see-through band in translucent bubbles like Live Caption.

This CL makes SolidColorAnalyzer bail on kDifference RRect clips, as
it already does for kDifference rect clips. It also reverts
crrev.com/c/8101578, which painted a second background on the contents
view, making the center nearly opaque and leaving a lighter 6 DIP ring
around it.

Fixed: 535315035

87fa779c66c84c2859707d91e78593b9f60e1571
parent
291b71f5fdcd23ec32bc4239472bb206254d0f9b [ diff ] 
Detach WebViewPlugin delegate in PluginPlaceholderBase::OnDestruct

When a RenderFrame is destroyed, PluginPlaceholder subclasses release
their self-reference and are destroyed. However, the associated
WebViewPlugin can outlive the RenderFrame if its WebPluginContainerImpl
is kept alive, for example by script across a layout tree reattach.

To prevent use-after-free issues from WebViewPlugin queued tasks or
Destroy() accessing a destroyed delegate, this adds
WebViewPlugin::DetachDelegate() and calls it during
PluginPlaceholderBase::OnDestruct(). Subclasses are also updated to
properly invoke the base class OnDestruct().

BUG=573981230

[Actor] Register ScriptToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest subclasses
expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and ToolRegistry
discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

This CL registers ScriptToolRequest in ToolRegistry by adding
ToolId::kScript, declaring kModelFacingName ("script"), and implementing
GetToolDefinition() with required document_identifier, tool_name, and
input_arguments parameters. It also adds unit test coverage in
tool_registry_unittest.cc.

Reuse initialized class tokens for shared HTML element data

Existing optimizations exist to share HTML element data, indicated by
!GetElementData()->IsUnique(). When element data is shared, we still end
up creating a SpaceSplitString for the value of the "class" attribute,
even though the shared version is available. This change avoids this
extra work. A similar optimization already exists in
Element::SetInlineStyleFromString.

Exceptions are made for non-HTML elements (as SVG elements may animate
className, making sharing unsafe), and uppercase class name values, as
they behave differently in quirks mode.

This change alone improves Speedometer3 performance by 0.1%. The
TodoMVC-jQuery test alone calls ClassAttributeChange 910,990 times with
a 99% reuse hit-rate due to heavy use of innerHTML.

[Actor] Register MoveMouseToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest subclasses
expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and ToolRegistry
discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

This CL registers MoveMouseToolRequest in ToolRegistry by adding
ToolId::kMoveMouse, declaring kModelFacingName ("move_mouse"), and
implementing GetToolDefinition() with required dom_node_id parameter.
It also adds unit test coverage in tool_registry_unittest.cc.

Fri Oct 16 22:53:33 2026
tree
3cf68eb12bccf26a1f6239a7b9d9149354ed6a6c
parent
483fd65f43e8fcb37ecb6f3d404af2b0482a147a [ diff ] 
[content] Drop unused mojom_platform_blink dep from content/browser

content/browser only uses the non-Blink variant of
//third_party/blink/public/mojom (e.g. input_handler.mojom.h), which it
already gets through //third_party/blink/public/common. The Android-only
dependency on mojom_platform_blink pulls the Blink-variant mojom objects
into every target that links content/browser. Those objects reference
Blink platform symbols (blink::KURL, blink::String traits,
blink::BlobDataHandle, ...) that are only linked together with Blink, so
standalone targets such as the Android libfuzzer fuzzer libraries fail
to link in non-component builds.

parent
ae6573c37ad334448b4fdfab958d740aa81d9699 [ diff ] 
[Dictation] Register a global hotkey to stop voice typing while listening

While voice typing is listening, the user can now stop it with the
Dictation hotkey from anywhere: another Chrome window, or another app.

- LocalHotkeyManager registers the hotkey OS-wide (ScopedGlobalHotkey)
  only while a stream is initializing or transcribing, and only while
  hotkeys are enabled (onboarded, not disabled by policy).
- The OS-wide hotkey can only stop dictation, and registration is best
  effort.
- ToggleHotkeyHandler() now stops the active stream when there is no
  focused editable to dictate into (e.g. in another Chrome window),
  instead of doing nothing.
- DictationKeyedService only forwards session state changes to
  LocalHotkeyManager.

Renaming it to HotkeyManager is left for a follow-up.

0a806ec631e676c243263f36fc86adb65601a941 [ diff ] 
🌺 [Android] Isolate filename in RequestConfirmation_AndroidDesktop

In ChromeDownloadManagerDelegateTest.RequestConfirmation_AndroidDesktop,
use a unique UUID-suffixed filename instead of a hardcoded "foo.txt". On
Android 10+, FileUtils.getIntermediateUriForPublicDownload queries and
inserts into the system MediaStore.Downloads provider, which persists
across test shards on physical devices when a previous run crashes
before test teardown cleans up the URI, causing the subsequent shard's
conflict test to observe a triple conflict (foo (2).txt) instead of the
expected single conflict (foo (1).txt).
